<script src="../app.js"></script>
<script>
const agents={
  byewind:{name:"ByeWind",email:"byewind@twitter.com",phone:"+41 0092 293 2903",initials:"BW",company:"Independent",availability:"Available",specializations:["Residential","Property Management"],properties:8,completedJobs:12,totalSpend:"£1,485",activeJobs:2},
  kate:{name:"Kate Morrison",email:"melody@altbox.com",phone:"+41 0092 293 2903",initials:"KM",company:"Morrison Property Services",availability:"Available",specializations:["Residential","Leasing"],properties:5,completedJobs:9,totalSpend:"£980",activeJobs:1},
  drew:{name:"Drew Cano",email:"max@kt.com",phone:"+41 0092 293 2903",initials:"DC",company:"KT Property Group",availability:"Limited availability",specializations:["Commercial","Property Management"],properties:11,completedJobs:18,totalSpend:"£2,340",activeJobs:3},
  orlando:{name:"Orlando Diggs",email:"sean@dellito.com",phone:"+41 0092 293 2903",initials:"OD",company:"Diggs Homes",availability:"Available",specializations:["Residential","Maintenance"],properties:6,completedJobs:14,totalSpend:"£1,125",activeJobs:0}
};

const id=new URLSearchParams(location.search).get("id")||"byewind";
const agent=agents[id]||agents.byewind;

document.getElementById("detailAvatar").querySelector("span").textContent=agent.initials;
document.getElementById("detailName").textContent=agent.name;
document.getElementById("detailCompany").textContent=agent.company;
document.getElementById("detailEmail").textContent=agent.email;
document.getElementById("detailPhone").textContent=agent.phone;
document.getElementById("detailAvailability").textContent=agent.availability;
document.getElementById("detailsAvailability").textContent=agent.availability;
document.getElementById("detailsProperties").textContent=agent.properties;
document.getElementById("detailsJobs").textContent=agent.completedJobs;
document.getElementById("detailsSpend").textContent=agent.totalSpend;
document.getElementById("activeJobsCount").textContent=`${agent.activeJobs} active`;
document.getElementById("detailSpecialization").innerHTML=agent.specializations.map(x=>`<span class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 text-md">${x}</span>`).join("");
document.getElementById("activeJobs").classList.toggle("hidden",agent.activeJobs===0);

document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded",()=>{
  const page=document.getElementById("page");
  if(page)requestAnimationFrame(()=>page.classList.remove("opacity-0","scale-[0.96]"));
});
</script>
</body>

<script src="../app.js"></script>
<script>
const agents=[
{id:"byewind",name:"ByeWind",email:"byewind@twitter.com",phone:"+41 0092 293 2903",initials:"BW",company:"Independent",availability:"Available",specializations:["Residential","Property Management"],properties:8,completedJobs:12,totalSpend:"£1,485"},
{id:"kate",name:"Kate Morrison",email:"melody@altbox.com",phone:"+41 0092 293 2903",initials:"KM",company:"Morrison Property Services",availability:"Available",specializations:["Residential","Leasing"],properties:5,completedJobs:9,totalSpend:"£980"},
{id:"drew",name:"Drew Cano",email:"max@kt.com",phone:"+41 0092 293 2903",initials:"DC",company:"KT Property Group",availability:"Limited availability",specializations:["Commercial","Property Management"],properties:11,completedJobs:18,totalSpend:"£2,340"},
{id:"orlando",name:"Orlando Diggs",email:"sean@dellito.com",phone:"+41 0092 293 2903",initials:"OD",company:"Diggs Homes",availability:"Available",specializations:["Residential","Maintenance"],properties:6,completedJobs:14,totalSpend:"£1,125"}];

const table=document.getElementById("agentTable"),search=document.getElementById("agentSearch"),filter=document.getElementById("availabilityFilter");

function renderAgents(){
  const q=search.value.toLowerCase().trim(),f=filter.value;
  const rows=agents.filter(a=>(!q||[a.name,a.email,a.company].some(v=>v.toLowerCase().includes(q)))&&(f==="All Availability"||a.availability===f));

  table.innerHTML=rows.length?rows.map(a=>`
    <a href="agent-view.html?id=${a.id}" class="block group">
      <div class="grid grid-cols-[1.2fr_1.35fr_1.2fr_.9fr_.65fr_.7fr_.8fr] gap-3 px-2 py-4 items-center text-sm border-b border-slate-100 group-hover:bg-[#FAF4DF]/50 transition-colors">
        <div class="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
          <div class="w-9 h-9 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500">${a.initials}</div>
          <div class="min-w-0">
            <p class="text-md font-medium text-slate-800 group-hover:text-[#1D3639] truncate">${a.name}</p>
            <p class="text-xs text-slate-400 truncate">${a.specializations.join(" · ")}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="text-md text-slate-700 truncate">${a.company}</div>
        <div class="leading-tight text-slate-600"><div>${a.phone}</div><div class="text-slate-400 truncate">${a.email}</div></div>
        <div><span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full ${a.availability==="Available"?"bg-[#C3DF93]/40 text-[#1D3639]":a.availability==="Limited availability"?"bg-[#FAF4DF] text-slate-700":"bg-slate-100 text-slate-500"} text-xs font-medium"><span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full ${a.availability==="Available"?"bg-[#1D3639]":a.availability==="Limited availability"?"bg-[#E3572B]":"bg-slate-400"}"></span>${a.availability}</span></div>
        <div>${a.properties}</div>
        <div>${a.completedJobs}</div>
        <div class="text-right font-medium">${a.totalSpend}</div>
      </div>
    </a>`).join(""):`<div class="py-10 text-center text-md text-slate-400">No agents found.</div>`;
}

async function loadFragment(id, file) {
  const target = document.getElementById(id);
  try {
    console.log("Loading fragment:", file);
    const response = await fetch(file);
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error("Could not load " + file);
    target.innerHTML = await response.text();
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);
  }
}

// get current url
const currentUrl = window.location.href;
console.log("Current URL:", currentUrl);
// check if it contains localhost
const isLocalhost = currentUrl.includes("localhost");
let baseUrl = "";
if (isLocalhost) {
    baseUrl = "http://localhost/yland/landlord/";
}else{
    baseUrl = "https://boluwarin001.github.io/yland/landlord/";
}


async function initLayout() {
  await Promise.all([
    loadFragment("sidebarMount", baseUrl + "sidebar.html"),
    loadFragment("headerMount", baseUrl + "header.html"),
    loadFragment("notificationsMount", baseUrl + "notifications.html")
  ]);

  const sidebar = document.getElementById("sidebar");
  const backdrop = document.getElementById("sidebarBackdrop");

  document.getElementById("sidebarToggle")?.addEventListener("click", () => {
    sidebar?.classList.toggle("-translate-x-full");
    backdrop?.classList.toggle("hidden");
  });

  backdrop?.addEventListener("click", () => {
    sidebar?.classList.add("-translate-x-full");
    backdrop?.classList.add("hidden");
  });

  const notifications = document.getElementById("notificationsPanel");
  document.getElementById("notificationButton")?.addEventListener("click", () => {
    notifications?.classList.toggle("translate-x-full");
  });

  document.querySelectorAll(".sidebarHref").forEach(href => {
    href.href = baseUrl + href.getAttribute("href");
    if (href.href === currentUrl) href.classList.add("bg-slate-100", "text-slate-800");
  });

  // Portal Switcher
  // Portal switcher logic with pop animation
  const portalSwitcher = document.getElementById("portalSwitcher");
  const portalMenu = document.getElementById("portalMenu");
  const portalChevron = document.getElementById("portalChevron");
  const currentPortal = document.getElementById("currentPortal");

  // Helper function to animate pop open / pop close
  function togglePortalMenu(show) {
    if (!portalMenu) return;
    const shouldOpen = show ?? portalMenu.classList.contains("invisible");

    if (shouldOpen) {
      portalMenu.classList.remove("opacity-0", "scale-95", "pointer-events-none", "invisible");
      portalMenu.classList.add("opacity-100", "scale-100");
      portalChevron?.classList.add("rotate-180");
    } else {
      portalMenu.classList.remove("opacity-100", "scale-100");
      portalMenu.classList.add("opacity-0", "scale-95", "pointer-events-none", "invisible");
      portalChevron?.classList.remove("rotate-180");
    }
  }

  portalSwitcher?.addEventListener("click", e => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    togglePortalMenu();
  });

  document.querySelectorAll(".portalOption").forEach(option => {
    option.addEventListener("click", () => {
      localStorage.setItem("activePortal", option.dataset.portal);
      // Use existing smooth page transition function if available
      const targetUrl = baseUrl + option.dataset.url;
      if (typeof navigateWithTransition === "function") {
        navigateWithTransition(targetUrl);
      } else {
        window.location.href = targetUrl;
      }
    });
  });

  const activePortal = localStorage.getItem("activePortal");

  if (activePortal && currentPortal) {
    currentPortal.textContent = activePortal;
    document.querySelectorAll(".portalOption").forEach(option => {
      option.querySelector(".portalCheck")?.classList.toggle("hidden", option.dataset.portal !== activePortal);
    });
  }

  // Close when clicking outside
  document.addEventListener("click", e => {
    if (!portalSwitcher?.contains(e.target) && !portalMenu?.contains(e.target)) {
      togglePortalMenu(false);
    }
  });

}

initLayout();

document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
  const page = document.getElementById("page");
  if (!page) return;

  page.style.opacity = "0";
  page.style.transform = "scale(0.96)";
  page.style.transition = "opacity 200ms ease, transform 200ms ease";

  requestAnimationFrame(() => {
    page.style.opacity = "1";
    page.style.transform = "scale(1)";
  });

});

function navigateWithTransition(url) {
  const page = document.getElementById("page");

  if (!page) { window.location.href = url; return;}
  // Start transition out
  page.style.opacity = "0";
  page.style.transform = "scale(0.96)";
  page.style.transition = "opacity 100ms ease, transform 100ms ease";

  setTimeout(() => {
    window.location.href = url;
  }, 100);
}


// Intercept normal left-click navigation
document.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
  // Only handle normal left-clicks
  if (event.button !== 0) return;
  // Don't interfere with Ctrl/Cmd/Shift/Alt clicks
  if ( event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey || event.shiftKey ||event.altKey) { return; }
  // Find the nearest <a>
  const anchor = event.target.closest("a");
  if (!anchor) return;
  const href = anchor.getAttribute("href");
  // Ignore links without destinations
  if (!href || href === "#") return;
  // Ignore downloads
  if (anchor.hasAttribute("download")) return;
  // Ignore external links
  const url = new URL(anchor.href, window.location.href);
  if (url.origin !== window.location.origin) {return;}
  // Ignore links that only change the hash
  if (url.pathname === window.location.pathname && url.search === window.location.search && url.hash) { return;}
  event.preventDefault();
  navigateWithTransition(url.href);

});

document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", () => {
  document.getElementById('page').style.opacity = 1;
  document.getElementById('page').style.transform = "translateX(0)";

                    </svg>

                  <div class="flex-1"><p class="text-xs font-medium">Boiler inspection</p><p class="text-[10px] text-slate-400">Kingslandlord Homes</p></div>

<script>
const requests = {
  'MR-1042': {id:'MR-1042',status:'Awaiting approval',title:'Leaking kitchen sink',property:'4B Villareal, Flat 15',description:'Water is leaking from the kitchen sink and pooling underneath the cabinet.',tenant:'Sarah Johnson',priority:'High',date:'Sep 9, 2026',agent:null},
  'MR-1041': {id:'MR-1041',status:'Awaiting approval',title:'Ceiling crack',property:'4B Villareal, Flat 15',description:'A visible crack has appeared across the bedroom ceiling.',tenant:'Michael Smith',priority:'High',date:'Sep 8, 2026',agent:null},
  'MR-1037': {id:'MR-1037',status:'In progress',title:'Boiler inspection',property:'4B Villareal, Flat 15',description:'The boiler is making unusual noises and needs to be inspected.',tenant:'Sarah Johnson',priority:'High',date:'Sep 6, 2026',agent:'ByeWind'},
  'MR-1035': {id:'MR-1035',status:'In progress',title:'Bedroom radiator',property:'17 Bagwell Avenue',description:'Radiator is not heating properly.',tenant:'James Brown',priority:'Low',date:'Sep 5, 2026',agent:'Drew Cano'},
  'MR-1028': {id:'MR-1028',status:'Completed',title:'Ceiling crack',property:'4B Villareal, Flat 15',description:'Bedroom ceiling crack repaired and inspected.',tenant:'Michael Smith',priority:'High',date:'Aug 28, 2026',agent:'ByeWind'}
};

const request = requests[new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('id')] || requests['MR-1042'];

const fieldMap = [
  ['requestId', request.id],
  ['requestTitle', request.title],
  ['requestProperty', request.property],
  ['detailDescription', request.description],
  ['detailTenant', request.tenant],
  ['detailDate', request.date],
  ['detailPriority', request.priority],
  ['detailProperty', request.property],
  ['summaryPriority', request.priority],
  ['summaryUpdated', request.date]
];

fieldMap.forEach(([id, value]) => {
  document.getElementById(id).textContent = value;
});

const statusEl = document.getElementById('requestStatus');
const summaryEl = document.getElementById('summaryStatus');
const summaryAgent = document.getElementById('summaryAgent');

function setStatus(s) {
  statusEl.textContent = s;
  summaryEl.textContent = s;
  statusEl.className = 'px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-md ' + (
    s === 'Approved' ? 'bg-green-50 text-green-700'
    : s === 'Rejected' ? 'bg-red-50 text-red-700'
    : s === 'In progress' ? 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700'
    : 'bg-yellow-50 text-yellow-700'
  );
}

function showAgent(agent) {
  summaryAgent.textContent = agent || 'Not assigned';
  document.getElementById('agentStatus').textContent = agent ? 'Working' : 'Not assigned';

  document.getElementById('agentProgress').innerHTML = agent
    ? `<div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
         <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
           <div class="w-11 h-11 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 flex items-center justify-center text-md font-semibold">${agent.split(' ').map(x => x[0]).join('')}</div>
           <div>
             <p class="text-md font-medium">${agent}</p>
             <p class="text-md text-slate-400">Assigned to this request</p>
           </div>
         </div>
         <span class="text-md text-slate-500">In progress</span>
       </div>
       <div class="mt-5">
         <div class="flex justify-between text-md mb-2"><span class="text-slate-400">Progress</span><span>35%</span></div>
         <div class="h-1.5 bg-slate-100 rounded-full"><div class="h-full w-[35%] bg-slate-900 rounded-full"></div></div>
       </div>
       <div class="mt-5 grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
         <div class="bg-slate-50 rounded-lg p-2.5 text-center text-md">Assigned</div>
         <div class="bg-slate-50 rounded-lg p-2.5 text-center text-md">2 Updates</div>
         <div class="bg-slate-50 rounded-lg p-2.5 text-center text-md">35%</div>
       </div>`
    : `<div class="flex items-center gap-3">
         <div class="w-11 h-11 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 flex items-center justify-center text-md text-slate-400">—</div>
         <div>
           <p class="text-md font-medium">No agent assigned</p>
           <p class="text-md text-slate-400">Assign an agent to begin work.</p>
         </div>
       </div>`;
}

setStatus(request.status);
showAgent(request.agent);

if (request.agent) {
  document.getElementById('agentSelect').value = request.agent;
}

document.getElementById('approveBtn').onclick = () => setStatus('Approved');
document.getElementById('rejectBtn').onclick = () => setStatus('Rejected');

document.getElementById('assignBtn').onclick = () => {
  const a = document.getElementById('agentSelect').value;
  if (a) {
    showAgent(a);
    setStatus('In progress');
  }
};

document.getElementById('commentForm').onsubmit = e => {
  e.preventDefault();
  const input = document.getElementById('commentInput');
  const text = input.value.trim();
  if (!text) return;

  const safe = text.replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;');

  document.getElementById('commentsList').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',
    `<div class="flex gap-3">
       <div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-slate-100 flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] font-semibold shrink-0">YL</div>
       <div>
         <div class="flex gap-2">
           <p class="text-md font-medium">You</p>
           <span class="text-md text-slate-400">Just now</span>
         </div>
         <p class="text-md text-slate-500 mt-1">${safe}</p>
       </div>
     </div>`);

  input.value = '';
};
</script>
</body>

<script src="../app.js"></script>
<script>
const requests = [
  {id:"MR-1042",status:"yet-to-start",property:"4B Villareal, Flat 15",title:"Leaking kitchen sink",description:"Water is leaking from the kitchen sink and pooling underneath the cabinet.",tenant:"Sarah Johnson",priority:"High",date:"Sep 9, 2026",agent:null},
  {id:"MR-1041",status:"yet-to-start",property:"4B Villareal, Flat 15",title:"Ceiling crack",description:"A visible crack has appeared across the bedroom ceiling.",tenant:"Michael Smith",priority:"High",date:"Sep 8, 2026",agent:null},
  {id:"MR-1039",status:"yet-to-start",property:"12 Larry Street",title:"Broken bathroom tap",description:"The bathroom tap is leaking continuously.",tenant:"David Williams",priority:"Low",date:"Sep 7, 2026",agent:null},
  {id:"MR-1037",status:"in-progress",property:"4B Villareal, Flat 15",title:"Boiler inspection",description:"The boiler is making unusual noises and needs to be inspected.",tenant:"Sarah Johnson",priority:"High",date:"Sep 6, 2026",agent:"ByeWind"},
  {id:"MR-1035",status:"in-progress",property:"17 Bagwell Avenue",title:"Bedroom radiator",description:"Radiator is not heating properly.",tenant:"James Brown",priority:"Low",date:"Sep 5, 2026",agent:"Drew Cano"},
  {id:"MR-1032",status:"in-progress",property:"8 Morrison Road",title:"Front door lock",description:"The front door lock is becoming difficult to operate.",tenant:"Emily Davis",priority:"Medium",date:"Sep 3, 2026",agent:"Kate Morrison"},
  {id:"MR-1028",status:"completed",property:"4B Villareal, Flat 15",title:"Ceiling crack",description:"Bedroom ceiling crack repaired and inspected.",tenant:"Michael Smith",priority:"High",date:"Aug 28, 2026",agent:"ByeWind"},
  {id:"MR-1024",status:"completed",property:"12 Larry Street",title:"Bathroom tap",description:"Bathroom tap replaced successfully.",tenant:"David Williams",priority:"Low",date:"Aug 24, 2026",agent:"Kate Morrison"},
  {id:"MR-1020",status:"completed",property:"5 Meadow Lane",title:"Light switches",description:"Faulty light switches replaced.",tenant:"Rachel Wilson",priority:"Low",date:"Aug 20, 2026",agent:"Orlando Diggs"}
];

const listIds = {
  'yet-to-start': 'yetToStartList',
  'in-progress': 'inProgressList',
  'completed': 'completedList'
};

function card(r) {
  const dot = r.status === 'yet-to-start' ? 'bg-yellow-300'
    : r.status === 'in-progress' ? 'bg-blue-400'
    : 'bg-green-400';

  const priority = r.priority === 'High' ? 'text-red-500'
    : r.priority === 'Medium' ? 'text-yellow-600'
    : 'text-slate-400';

  const initials = r.tenant.split(' ').map(x => x[0]).join('').slice(0, 2);

  return `<button type="button" data-id="${r.id}" class="request-card w-full text-left bg-slate-50 rounded-xl p-4 hover:bg-slate-100 transition">
    <div class="flex justify-between gap-3">
      <span class="text-md text-slate-400 truncate">${r.property}</span>
      <span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${dot} mt-1.5 shrink-0"></span>
    </div>
    <p class="text-md font-semibold mt-2.5">${r.title}</p>
    <p class="text-md text-slate-400 mt-1 line-clamp-2">${r.description}</p>
    <div class="flex justify-between items-center mt-4">
      <div class="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
        <span class="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-slate-200 flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] font-semibold">${initials}</span>
        <span class="text-md text-slate-500 truncate">${r.tenant}</span>
      </div>
      <span class="text-md ${priority}">${r.priority}</span>
    </div>
  </button>`;
}

function render() {
  Object.entries(listIds).forEach(([status, id]) => {
    document.getElementById(id).innerHTML = requests.filter(r => r.status === status).map(card).join('');
  });

  document.querySelectorAll('.request-card').forEach(c => {
    c.onclick = () => location.href = `maintenance-item.html?id=${c.dataset.id}`;
  });
}

render();

const filterIdle = 'filter-btn px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 text-slate-500 text-md whitespace-nowrap';
const filterActive = 'filter-btn px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-900 text-white text-md whitespace-nowrap';

document.querySelectorAll('.filter-btn').forEach(btn => {
  btn.onclick = () => {
    document.querySelectorAll('.filter-btn').forEach(x => x.className = filterIdle);
    btn.className = filterActive;

    document.querySelectorAll('#requestBoard section').forEach(section => {
      section.classList.toggle(
        'hidden',
        btn.dataset.filter !== 'all' && section.dataset.column !== btn.dataset.filter
      );
    });
  };
});
</script>
</body>

<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded",()=>{
  const form=document.getElementById("propertyForm");
  form.addEventListener("submit",e=>{
    e.preventDefault();
    if(!form.checkValidity()){form.reportValidity();return;}
    const button=form.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
    button.disabled=true;
    button.innerHTML='<svg class="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9" stroke-width="1.7" class="opacity-30"/><path stroke-width="1.7" stroke-linecap="round" d="M21 12a9 9 0 0 0-9-9"/></svg> Creating...';
    setTimeout(()=>{
      window.location.href="property-index.html";
    },700);
  });
});
</script>

<script src="../app.js"></script>
<script src="property.js"></script>

<script>

const mediaInput=document.getElementById("propertyMedia");
const mediaUploadButton=document.getElementById("mediaUploadButton");
const mediaPreview=document.getElementById("mediaPreview");
let selectedMedia=[];

mediaUploadButton.addEventListener("click",()=>mediaInput.click());

mediaInput.addEventListener("change",event=>{
  addMedia(Array.from(event.target.files));
  mediaInput.value="";
});

function addMedia(files){
  const images=files.filter(file=>file.type.startsWith("image/"));
  selectedMedia=[...selectedMedia,...images];
  renderMedia();
}

function renderMedia(){

  mediaPreview.innerHTML="";

  selectedMedia.forEach((file,index)=>{

    const url=URL.createObjectURL(file);
    const card=document.createElement("div");

    card.className="relative group rounded-xl overflow-hidden bg-slate-100 aspect-[4/3] border border-slate-200";

    card.innerHTML=`
      <img src="${url}" alt="${file.name}" class="w-full h-full object-cover">

      <button type="button"
        data-media-index="${index}"
        class="absolute top-2 right-2 w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-white/90 text-slate-600 shadow-sm opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-white"
        title="Remove image">

        <svg class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
          <path stroke-width="1.7" stroke-linecap="round" d="m7 7 10 10M17 7 7 17"/>
        </svg>

      </button>

      <div class="absolute bottom-0 inset-x-0 px-2.5 py-2 bg-gradient-to-t from-black/60 to-transparent">
        <p class="text-[11px] text-white truncate">${file.name}</p>
      </div>
    `;

    card.querySelector("button").addEventListener("click",()=>{

      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      selectedMedia.splice(index,1);
      renderMedia();

    });

    mediaPreview.appendChild(card);

  });

}


mediaUploadButton.addEventListener("dragover",event=>{

  event.preventDefault();

  mediaUploadButton.classList.add(
    "border-[#1D3639]",
    "bg-[#FAF4DF]"
  );

});


mediaUploadButton.addEventListener("dragleave",()=>{

  mediaUploadButton.classList.remove(
    "border-[#1D3639]",
    "bg-[#FAF4DF]"
  );

});


mediaUploadButton.addEventListener("drop",event=>{

  event.preventDefault();

  mediaUploadButton.classList.remove(
    "border-[#1D3639]",
    "bg-[#FAF4DF]"
  );

  addMedia(Array.from(event.dataTransfer.files));

});


document.getElementById("copyApplicationLink").addEventListener("click",async()=>{

  const input=document.querySelector('[name="application_link"]');

  try{

    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(input.value);

    const button=document.getElementById("copyApplicationLink");

    button.innerHTML=`
      <svg class="w-4 h-4 text-[#1D3639]" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
        <path stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="m5 12 4 4L19 6"/>
      </svg>
    `;

    setTimeout(()=>{
      button.innerHTML=`
        <svg class="w-4 h-4 text-slate-500" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
          <rect x="8" y="8" width="11" height="11" rx="2" stroke-width="1.7"/>
          <path d="M16 8V6a2 2 0 00-2-2H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v8a2 2 0 002 2h2" stroke-width="1.7"/>
        </svg>
      `;
    },1500);

  }catch(error){
    console.error(error);
  }

});


document.getElementById("propertyForm").addEventListener("submit",event=>{

  event.preventDefault();

  const formData=new FormData(event.target);
  const property=Object.fromEntries(formData.entries());

  property.features=formData.getAll("features[]");
  property.media=selectedMedia;

  selectedMedia.forEach(file=>{
    formData.append("media[]",file);
  });

  console.log(property,formData);

  const message=document.getElementById("formMessage");

  message.textContent="Property details saved.";
  message.className="text-xs text-emerald-600";

  setTimeout(()=>{
    message.textContent="";
  },3000);

});


window.addEventListener("load",()=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
  document.getElementById("page").classList.remove("opacity-0");
}));

</script>

function documentCard(doc) {
  return `
    <div class="bg-orange-50 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-4 sm:px-5 py-4 hover:border-slate-200 transition">
      <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center gap-4">
        <div class="flex items-start gap-3 min-w-0 flex-1">
          <div class="w-10 h-10 sm:w-11 sm:h-11 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-center text-slate-400 shrink-0">
            <svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M7 3h7l4 4v14H7V3Zm7 1v4h4"/></svg>
          </div>

          <div class="min-w-0 flex-1">
            <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
              <p class="text-md font-medium break-words">${doc.name}</p>
              <span class="shrink-0 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-slate-100 text-[11px] text-slate-500">v${doc.version}</span>
              ${doc.tenantVisible ? `<span class="shrink-0 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-emerald-50 text-[11px] text-emerald-600">Tenant visible</span>` : ""}
            </div>
            <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-3 gap-y-1 mt-1">
              <span class="text-md text-slate-400">${doc.date}</span>
              <span class="text-md text-slate-400">Uploaded by ${doc.uploadedBy}</span>
              <span class="text-md text-slate-400">${doc.size}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1 sm:justify-end sm:shrink-0 border-t border-slate-100 sm:border-0 pt-3 sm:pt-0">
          <button type="button" data-action="tenant" data-id="${doc.id}" class="h-9 px-2.5 sm:px-3 rounded-lg text-md ${doc.tenantVisible ? "text-emerald-600 bg-emerald-50" : "text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-50"}" title="${doc.tenantVisible ? "Hide from tenant" : "Show to tenant"}">
            <span class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <svg class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M2.5 12s3.5-5 9.5-5 9.5 5 9.5 5-3.5 5-9.5 5-9.5-5-9.5-5Z"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="2.2" stroke-width="1.5"/></svg>
              <span class="hidden sm:inline">${doc.tenantVisible ? "Shown to Tenant" : "Show to Tenant"}</span>
            </span>
          </button>
          <button type="button" data-action="versions" data-id="${doc.id}" class="h-9 px-2.5 sm:px-3 rounded-lg text-md text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-50">Versions ${doc.versions.length > 1 ? `(${doc.versions.length})` : ""}</button>
          <button type="button" data-action="new-version" data-id="${doc.id}" class="h-9 px-2.5 sm:px-3 rounded-lg text-md text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-50">+ Version</button>
          <button type="button" data-action="download" data-id="${doc.id}" class="w-9 h-9 rounded-lg text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-50 flex items-center justify-center" title="Download">
            <svg class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M12 4v11m0 0 4-4m-4 4-4-4M5 20h14"/></svg>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
}
function renderDocuments() {
  const search = document.getElementById("documentSearch").value.trim().toLowerCase();
  const category = document.getElementById("categoryFilter").value;
  const filtered = documents.filter(doc => {
    const matchesSearch = !search || `${doc.name} ${doc.category} ${doc.uploadedBy}`.toLowerCase().includes(search);
    return matchesSearch && (category === "all" || doc.category === category);
  });

  document.getElementById("documentCount").textContent = `${filtered.length} document${filtered.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;

  document.getElementById("documentGroups").innerHTML = Object.keys(documentTypes).map(categoryName => {
    const docs = filtered.filter(doc => doc.category === categoryName);
    if (!docs.length) return "";

    return `
      <section class="mb-20">
        <div class="flex items-center gap-3 mb-3">
          <div class="w-9 h-9 rounded-lg bg-white border border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-center text-slate-400">${categoryIcons[categoryName]}</div>
          <div>
            <h3 class="text-md font-semibold">${categoryName}</h3>
            <p class="text-md text-slate-400">${docs.length} document${docs.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="space-y-2">${docs.map(documentCard).join("")}</div>
      </section>
    `;
  }).join("") || `<div class="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-200 bg-white p-12 text-center"><p class="text-md font-medium">No documents found</p><p class="text-md text-slate-400 mt-1">Try another search or upload a new document.</p></div>`;

  document.querySelectorAll("[data-action]").forEach(button => {
    button.onclick = () => {
      const doc = documents.find(x => x.id === button.dataset.id);
      if (!doc) return;
      if (button.dataset.action === "versions") showVersions(doc);
      if (button.dataset.action === "new-version") openUpload(doc);
      if (button.dataset.action === "download") alert(`Download: ${doc.file}`);
      if (button.dataset.action === "tenant") {
        doc.tenantVisible = !doc.tenantVisible;
        renderDocuments();
      }
    };
  });
}

function populateDocumentTypes(category, selected = "") {
  const type = document.getElementById("documentType");
  const options = category ? [...documentTypes[category], "Custom document name"] : [];
  type.innerHTML = `<option value="">Select document type</option>${options.map(x => `<option value="${x}">${x}</option>`).join("")}`;
  type.value = selected;
  document.getElementById("customNameWrap").classList.toggle("hidden", selected !== "Custom document name");
}

const modal = document.getElementById("documentModal");
const modalPanel = document.getElementById("documentModalPanel");
const versionModal = document.getElementById("versionModal");
const versionModalPanel = document.getElementById("versionModalPanel");
const existingWrap = document.getElementById("existingVersionWrap");
const existingSelect = document.getElementById("existingDocument");

function animateOpen(overlay, panel) {
  overlay.classList.remove("hidden");
  requestAnimationFrame(() => {
    overlay.classList.add("is-open");
    panel.classList.add("is-open");
  });
  document.body.classList.add("overflow-hidden");
}

function animateClose(overlay, panel, callback = null) {
  overlay.classList.remove("is-open");
  panel.classList.remove("is-open");
  setTimeout(() => {
    overlay.classList.add("hidden");
    if (callback) callback();
    if (modal.classList.contains("hidden") && versionModal.classList.contains("hidden")) document.body.classList.remove("overflow-hidden");
  }, 180);
}

function openModal() {
  animateOpen(modal, modalPanel);
}

function closeModal() {
  if (modal.classList.contains("hidden")) return;
  animateClose(modal, modalPanel, () => {
    document.getElementById("documentForm").reset();
    document.getElementById("documentType").innerHTML = `<option value="">Select document type</option>`;
    document.getElementById("customNameWrap").classList.add("hidden");
    document.getElementById("existingVersionWrap").classList.add("hidden");
    document.getElementById("fileName").textContent = "Choose a document";
  });
}

function openUpload(doc = null) {
  document.getElementById("documentForm").reset();
  document.getElementById("fileName").textContent = "Choose a document";
  document.getElementById("customNameWrap").classList.add("hidden");
  document.getElementById("documentCategory").value = doc ? doc.category : "";
  populateDocumentTypes(doc ? doc.category : "", doc ? doc.name : "");
  existingSelect.innerHTML = `<option value="">This is a new document</option>${documents.map(x => `<option value="${x.id}">${x.name} · current v${x.version}</option>`).join("")}`;
  existingSelect.value = doc ? doc.id : "";
  existingWrap.classList.toggle("hidden", !doc);
  openModal();
}

function showVersions(doc) {
  document.getElementById("versionModalTitle").textContent = doc.name;
  renderVersions(doc);
  animateOpen(versionModal, versionModalPanel);
}

function renderVersions(doc) {
  document.getElementById("versionList").innerHTML = doc.versions.map(v => `
    <div class="rounded-xl border border-slate-100 ${v.version === doc.version ? "bg-slate-50" : "bg-white"} p-4">
      <div class="flex items-center gap-4">
        <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-white border border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-center text-md font-semibold text-slate-500">v${v.version}</div>
        <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">
          <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
            <p class="text-md font-medium">${v.version === doc.version ? "Current version" : "Previous version"}</p>
            ${v.version === doc.version ? `<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-slate-200 text-[11px] text-slate-500">Current</span>` : ""}
          </div>
          <p class="text-md text-slate-400 mt-1">${v.date} · Uploaded by ${v.uploadedBy} · ${v.size}</p>
        </div>
        <div class="flex items-center gap-1">
          <button type="button" data-version-download="${v.version}" class="h-9 px-3 rounded-lg text-md text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-50">Download</button>
          <button type="button" data-version-delete="${v.version}" class="w-9 h-9 rounded-lg text-slate-400 hover:text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50 flex items-center justify-center" title="Delete version">
            <svg class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M6 7h12m-9 0V5h6v2m-7 3v7m4-7v7m4-7v7M5 7l1 13h12l1-13"/></svg>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  `).join("");

  document.querySelectorAll("[data-version-delete]").forEach(button => {
    button.onclick = () => deleteVersion(doc, Number(button.dataset.versionDelete));
  });

  document.querySelectorAll("[data-version-download]").forEach(button => {
    button.onclick = () => {
      const version = doc.versions.find(v => v.version === Number(button.dataset.versionDownload));
      if (version) alert(`Download: ${version.file}`);
    };
  });
}

function deleteVersion(doc, versionNumber) {
  const version = doc.versions.find(v => v.version === versionNumber);
  if (!version) return;

  const isCurrent = versionNumber === doc.version;
  const message = isCurrent
    ? doc.versions.length > 1
      ? `Delete v${versionNumber}? The previous version will become the current version.`
      : `Delete v${versionNumber}? This will remove the document entirely.`
    : `Delete previous version v${versionNumber}?`;

  if (!confirm(message)) return;

  doc.versions = doc.versions.filter(v => v.version !== versionNumber);

  if (!doc.versions.length) {
    const index = documents.findIndex(x => x.id === doc.id);
    if (index !== -1) documents.splice(index, 1);
    closeVersions();
    renderDocuments();
    return;
  }

  if (isCurrent) {
    const nextCurrent = doc.versions.reduce((latest, item) => item.version > latest.version ? item : latest, doc.versions[0]);
    doc.version = nextCurrent.version;
    doc.date = nextCurrent.date;
    doc.uploadedBy = nextCurrent.uploadedBy;
    doc.size = nextCurrent.size;
    doc.file = nextCurrent.file;
  }

  doc.versions.sort((a,b) => b.version - a.version);
  renderVersions(doc);
  renderDocuments();
}

function closeVersions() {
  if (versionModal.classList.contains("hidden")) return;
  animateClose(versionModal, versionModalPanel);
}

document.getElementById("uploadDocumentBtn").onclick = () => openUpload();
document.getElementById("closeDocumentModal").onclick = closeModal;
document.getElementById("cancelDocument").onclick = closeModal;


document.getElementById("documentCategory").onchange = e => {
  populateDocumentTypes(e.target.value);
  existingWrap.classList.add("hidden");
};

document.getElementById("documentType").onchange = e => {
  document.getElementById("customNameWrap").classList.toggle("hidden", e.target.value !== "Custom document name");
};

document.getElementById("existingDocument").onchange = e => {
  const doc = documents.find(x => x.id === e.target.value);
  if (doc) {
    document.getElementById("documentCategory").value = doc.category;
    populateDocumentTypes(doc.category, doc.name);
  }
};

document.getElementById("documentDropzone").onclick = () => document.getElementById("documentFile").click();
document.getElementById("documentFile").onchange = e => {
  document.getElementById("fileName").textContent = e.target.files[0]?.name || "Choose a document";
};

document.getElementById("documentForm").onsubmit = e => {
  e.preventDefault();

  const form = new FormData(e.target);
  const existingId = form.get("existing_document");
  const category = form.get("category");
  const type = form.get("document_type");
  const customName = form.get("custom_name");
  const name = type === "Custom document name" ? customName : type;
  const file = document.getElementById("documentFile").files[0];

  if (!name || !file) return;

  const date = new Date().toLocaleDateString("en-GB",{day:"2-digit",month:"short",year:"numeric"});
  const size = `${Math.max(1, Math.round(file.size / 1024))} KB`;

  if (existingId) {
    const doc = documents.find(x => x.id === existingId);
    doc.version++;
    doc.date = date;
    doc.uploadedBy = "You";
    doc.size = size;
    doc.file = file.name;
    doc.versions.unshift({version:doc.version,date,uploadedBy:doc.uploadedBy,size,file:file.name});
  } else {
    const id = `${Date.now()}`;
    documents.unshift({id,name,category,version:1,tenantVisible:false,date,uploadedBy:"You",size,file:file.name,versions:[{version:1,date,uploadedBy:"You",size,file:file.name}]});
  }

  closeModal();
  renderDocuments();
};

document.querySelectorAll("[data-action]");

document.getElementById("documentSearch").oninput = renderDocuments;
document.getElementById("categoryFilter").onchange = renderDocuments;

document.onkeydown = e => {
  if (e.key === "Escape") {
    if (!modal.classList.contains("hidden")) closeModal();
    else if (!versionModal.classList.contains("hidden")) closeVersions();
  }
};

renderDocuments();
</script>

  <script>
    const filters=document.querySelectorAll('.history-filter'),events=document.querySelectorAll('.history-event'),search=document.getElementById('historySearch'),dateFilter=document.getElementById('dateFilter'),empty=document.getElementById('emptyState');

    filters.forEach(btn=>btn.addEventListener('click',()=>{
      filters.forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('bg-[#1D3639]','text-white'));
      filters.forEach(b=>b.classList.add('bg-slate-100','text-slate-600'));
      btn.classList.remove('bg-slate-100','text-slate-600');
      btn.classList.add('bg-[#1D3639]','text-white');
      filterHistory();
    }));

    search.addEventListener('input',filterHistory);
    dateFilter.addEventListener('change',filterHistory);

    function filterHistory(){
      const category=document.querySelector('.history-filter.bg-\\[\\#1D3639\\]')?.dataset.filter||'all';
      const query=search.value.toLowerCase().trim();
      let visible=0;

      events.forEach(event=>{
        const matchesCategory=category==='all'||event.dataset.category===category;
        const matchesSearch=!query||event.dataset.search.toLowerCase().includes(query);
        const show=matchesCategory&&matchesSearch;
        event.classList.toggle('hidden',!show);
        if(show)visible++;
      });

      document.querySelectorAll('.history-day').forEach(day=>{
        const visibleEvents=[...day.querySelectorAll('.history-event:not(.hidden)')];
        day.classList.toggle('hidden',visibleEvents.length===0);
      });

      empty.classList.toggle('hidden',visible!==0);
    }

    window.addEventListener('load',()=>{
      const page=document.getElementById('page');
      requestAnimationFrame(()=>page.classList.remove('opacity-0'));
    });
  </script>

  const heroStatusBadge = document.getElementById("heroStatusBadge");

    },
    {

      action: "View Tenants",
      actionTab: "tenant",
      rentalValue: "£950",
      totalRentPaid: "£8,550",
      nextRentDue: "£950",
      nextRentDate: "11 March 2026",
      paymentStatus: "Rent collection active",
      applications: "1",
      applicationsNote: "No pending applications",
      maintenance: "2",
      maintenanceNote: "1 requires attention",
      units: "1",
      unitsNote: "1 currently occupied",
      occupancy: "100%",
      occupancyNote: "Tenant assigned"
    },
    {
      title: "Inactive / Paused",
      description: "This property has been paused and is not currently available for applications.",
      status: "Not public",
      statusClass: "bg-amber-500/90 text-white",
      dotClass: "bg-white",
      image: "../assets/images/property-paused.jpg",
      action: "Unpause Property",
      actionTab: "info",
      rentalValue: "£950",
      totalRentPaid: "£8,550",
      nextRentDue: "£0",
      nextRentDate: "No rent scheduled",
      paymentStatus: "Rent collection paused",
      applications: "0",
      applicationsNote: "Applications paused",
      maintenance: "1",
      maintenanceNote: "1 open request",
      units: "1",
      unitsNote: "Property paused",
      occupancy: "0%",
      occupancyNote: "Not available"
    }
  ];

  let currentStage = Math.floor(Math.random() * stages.length);

  const fields = {
    rentalValue: "rentalValue",
    totalRentPaid: "totalRentPaid",
    nextRentDue: "nextRentDue",
    nextRentDate: "nextRentDate",
    paymentStatus: "paymentStatus",
    applicationsCount: "applications",
    applicationsNote: "applicationsNote",
    maintenanceCount: "maintenance",
    maintenanceNote: "maintenanceNote",
    activeUnitsCount: "units",
    activeUnitsNote: "unitsNote",
    occupancyCount: "occupancy",
    occupancyNote: "occupancyNote"
  };

  function setText(id, value) {
    const element = document.getElementById(id);
    if (element) element.textContent = value;
  }

  function updateStage() {

    const stage = stages[currentStage];

    setText("heroStageTitle", stage.title);
    setText("heroStageDescription", stage.description);
    setText("heroStatusText", stage.status);
    setText("heroActionText", stage.action);

    hero.style.backgroundImage = `url('${stage.image}')`;

    heroStatusBadge.className = "inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium " + stage.statusClass;
    heroStatusDot.className = "w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full " + stage.dotClass;

    for (const [id, key] of Object.entries(fields)) {
      setText(id, stage[key]);
    }

    const label = stageButton.querySelector("[data-stage-label]");
    if (label) label.textContent = `Stage ${currentStage + 1}/4`;
  }

  stageButton.addEventListener("click", () => {
    currentStage = (currentStage + 1) % stages.length;
    updateStage();
  });

  heroAction.addEventListener("click", () => {
    const stage = stages[currentStage];
    if (stage.actionTab) {
      const tab = document.querySelector(`[data-tab="${stage.actionTab}"]`);
      if (tab) tab.click();
    }

  });

  updateStage();

});
</script>

const applications=[
{name:"Daniel Williams",submitted:"2 hours ago",moveIn:"1 Oct 2026",status:"Under Review"},
{name:"Sarah Thompson",submitted:"Yesterday",moveIn:"15 Oct 2026",status:"Under Review"},
{name:"Michael Brown",submitted:"2 days ago",moveIn:"1 Nov 2026",status:"Approved"},
{name:"Aisha Mohammed",submitted:"4 days ago",moveIn:"1 Oct 2026",status:"Pending"}
];

function renderInterests(){
  const search=document.getElementById("interestSearch").value.trim().toLowerCase();
  const filtered=interests.filter(x=>`${x.name} ${x.email} ${x.phone} ${x.contact} ${x.notes}`.toLowerCase().includes(search));
  document.getElementById("interestCount").textContent=filtered.length;
  document.getElementById("interestCountHeader").textContent=filtered.length;

  document.getElementById("interestTableBody").innerHTML=filtered.length?filtered.map(x=>{
    const i=interests.indexOf(x);
    return `<tr data-interest-index="${i}" class="group cursor-pointer hover:bg-[#FAF4DF]/40 transition">
      <td class="px-5 py-4"><span class="text-md text-slate-500 whitespace-nowrap">${x.submitted}</span></td>
      <td class="px-5 py-4"><p class="text-md font-medium whitespace-nowrap">${x.name}</p></td>
      <td class="px-5 py-4"><span class="text-md text-slate-500 whitespace-nowrap">${x.preferredViewing}</span></td>
      <td class="px-5 py-4 text-right"><svg class="w-4 h-4 ml-auto text-slate-300 group-hover:text-[#1D3639] transition" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="m9 18 6-6-6-6"/></svg></td>
    </tr>`;
  }).join(""):`<tr><td colspan="4" class="px-5 py-12 text-center text-md text-slate-400">No interests found.</td></tr>`;

  document.querySelectorAll("[data-interest-index]").forEach(row=>row.addEventListener("click",()=>openInterestModal(interests[Number(row.dataset.interestIndex)])));
}

function renderApplications(){
  document.getElementById("applicationCount").textContent=applications.length;

  document.getElementById("applicationTableBody").innerHTML=applications.map(x=>`
    <tr class="group cursor-pointer hover:bg-[#C3DF93]/20 transition">
      <td class="px-5 py-4 text-md text-slate-500 whitespace-nowrap">${x.submitted}</td>
      <td class="px-5 py-4 text-md font-medium whitespace-nowrap">${x.name}</td>
      <td class="px-5 py-4 text-md text-slate-500 whitespace-nowrap">${x.moveIn}</td>
      <td class="px-5 py-4"><span class="inline-flex px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 text-xs">${x.status}</span></td>
      <td class="px-5 py-4 text-right"><svg class="w-4 h-4 ml-auto text-slate-300 group-hover:text-[#1D3639] transition" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="m9 18 6-6-6-6"/></svg></td>
    </tr>
  `).join("");
}

function openInterestModal(x){
  document.getElementById("interestModalName").textContent=x.name;
  document.getElementById("interestName").textContent=x.name;
  document.getElementById("interestSubmitted").textContent=x.submitted;
  document.getElementById("interestEmail").textContent=x.email;
  document.getElementById("interestPhone").textContent=x.phone;
  document.getElementById("interestContact").textContent=x.contact;
  document.getElementById("interestViewing").textContent=x.viewing;
  document.getElementById("interestPreferredViewing").textContent=x.preferredViewing;
  document.getElementById("interestMoveIn").textContent=x.moveIn;
  document.getElementById("interestNotes").textContent=x.notes;
  document.getElementById("interestModal").classList.remove("hidden");
  document.body.classList.add("overflow-hidden");
}

function closeInterestModal(){
  document.getElementById("interestModal").classList.add("hidden");
  document.body.classList.remove("overflow-hidden");
}

document.getElementById("interestSearch").addEventListener("input",renderInterests);
document.getElementById("closeInterestModal").addEventListener("click",closeInterestModal);
document.getElementById("closeInterestModalBottom").addEventListener("click",closeInterestModal);
document.getElementById("interestModalBackdrop").addEventListener("click",closeInterestModal);
document.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{if(e.key==="Escape")closeInterestModal()});

const publicUrl="/showcase/property_id",applicationUrl="/apply/property_id";

document.getElementById("viewPublicLink").addEventListener("click",()=>window.open(publicUrl,"_blank"));

document.getElementById("copyPublicLink").addEventListener("click",async()=>{
  await navigator.clipboard.writeText(window.location.origin+publicUrl);
  const btn=document.getElementById("copyPublicLink");
  btn.innerHTML='<svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-width="1.7" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="m5 12 4 4L19 6"/></svg>';
  setTimeout(()=>btn.innerHTML='<svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><rect x="9" y="9" width="11" height="11" rx="2" stroke-width="1.5"/><path stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" d="M15 9V6a2 2 0 0 0-2-2H6a2 2 0 0 0-2 2v7a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h3"/></svg>',1500);
});

document.getElementById("disablePublicLink").addEventListener("click",()=>{if(confirm("Are you sure you want to disable the public property link?"))alert("Public link disabled.")});
document.getElementById("openApplicationLink").addEventListener("click",()=>window.open(applicationUrl,"_blank"));

document.getElementById("copyApplicationLink").addEventListener("click",async()=>{
  await navigator.clipboard.writeText(window.location.origin+applicationUrl);

  setTimeout(()=>btn.innerHTML='<svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><rect x="9" y="9" width="11" height="11" rx="2" stroke-width="1.5"/><path stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" d="M15 9V6a2 2 0 0 0-2-2H6a2 2 0 0 0-2 2v7a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h3"/></svg>',1500);
});

              class="text-md w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-4 py-3 outline-none focus:border-[#1D3639] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#1D3639]/10"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label class="mb-2 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">Type</label>
            <select
              id="itemType"
              onchange="toggleDocumentField()"
              class="text-md w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-4 py-3 outline-none focus:border-[#1D3639]"
            >
              <option>Action</option>
              <option>Read / Accept Document</option>
              <option>Upload Document</option>
              <option>Keys / Access</option>
              <option>Confirmation</option>
            </select>
          </div>

          <div id="documentField" class="hidden">
            <label class="mb-2 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">Document to Sign</label>
            <select
              id="itemDocument"
              class="text-md w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-4 py-3 outline-none focus:border-[#1D3639]"
            >
              <option value="">Select a document</option>
              <option value="Tenancy Agreement">Tenancy Agreement</option>
              <option value="Property Rules">Property Rules</option>
              <option value="Inventory">Inventory</option>
              <option value="Move In Instructions">Move In Instructions</option>
              <option value="Move Out Instructions">Move Out Instructions</option>
            </select>
            <p class="mt-2 text-xs text-slate-400">
              The tenant will be asked to read and accept this document.
            </p>
          </div>

          <label class="flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-3">
            <input id="itemRequired" type="checkbox" class="h-4 w-4 rounded border-slate-300" />
            <span class="text-md text-slate-700">This item is required</span>
          </label>

          <div class="flex justify-end gap-3 border-t border-slate-100 pt-5">
            <button
              type="button"
              onclick="closeModal()"
              class="text-md rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-slate-700"
            >
              Cancel
            </button>
            <button class="text-md rounded-xl bg-[#1D3639] px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-white">
              Save Item
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>

    <script src="../app.js"></script>
    <script src="property.js"></script>
    <script>
      /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
       * State
       * ---------------------------------------------------------------- */
      let editing = false;

      /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
       * Data
       * ---------------------------------------------------------------- */
      const DOCUMENTS = [
        'Tenancy Agreement',
        'Property Rules',
        'Inventory',
        'Move In Instructions',
        'Move Out Instructions',
      ];

      const checklists = {
        movein: [
          { id: 1, description: 'Read and accept tenancy agreement', type: 'Read / Accept Document', document: 'Tenancy Agreement', required: true, completed: true, date: '12 Sep 2026' },
          { id: 2, description: 'Read and accept property rules', type: 'Read / Accept Document', document: 'Property Rules', required: true, completed: true, date: '12 Sep 2026' },
          { id: 3, description: 'Complete tenant information', type: 'Action', document: null, required: true, completed: true, date: '13 Sep 2026' },
          { id: 4, description: 'Provide emergency contact', type: 'Confirmation', document: null, required: true, completed: true, date: '13 Sep 2026' },
          { id: 5, description: 'Receive property keys', type: 'Keys / Access', document: null, required: true, completed: true, date: '14 Sep 2026' },
          { id: 6, description: 'Complete inventory', type: 'Action', document: null, required: false, completed: false, date: null },
          { id: 7, description: 'Confirm meter readings', type: 'Confirmation', document: null, required: false, completed: false, date: null },
        ],
        moveout: [
          { id: 8, description: 'Give notice', type: 'Action', document: null, required: true, completed: true, date: '01 Sep 2026' },
          { id: 9, description: 'Read move-out instructions', type: 'Read / Accept Document', document: 'Move Out Instructions', required: true, completed: true, date: '02 Sep 2026' },
          { id: 10, description: 'Complete move-out inspection', type: 'Action', document: null, required: true, completed: false, date: null },
          { id: 11, description: 'Return property keys', type: 'Keys / Access', document: null, required: true, completed: false, date: null },
          { id: 12, description: 'Provide final meter readings', type: 'Confirmation', document: null, required: false, completed: false, date: null },
          { id: 13, description: 'Confirm forwarding address', type: 'Confirmation', document: null, required: false, completed: true, date: '05 Sep 2026' },
        ],
      };

      const uploads = [
        { name: 'Proof of ID.pdf', type: 'Identity Document', date: '12 Sep 2026', size: '2.4 MB' },
        { name: 'Emergency Contact Form.pdf', type: 'Tenant Information', date: '13 Sep 2026', size: '418 KB' },
        { name: 'Meter Readings.jpg', type: 'Property Information', date: '14 Sep 2026', size: '1.8 MB' },
        { name: 'Signed Inventory.pdf', type: 'Inventory', date: '15 Sep 2026', size: '3.1 MB' },
      ];

      /** Maps each checklist key to its DOM id prefixes. */
      const LISTS = {
        movein: { prefix: 'moveIn', editTarget: 'editMoveIn', label: 'Move In' },
        moveout: { prefix: 'moveOut', editTarget: 'editMoveOut', label: 'Move Out' },
      };

      /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
       * Shared class strings
       * ---------------------------------------------------------------- */
      const BADGE = {
        complete: 'rounded-full bg-[#C3DF93]/40 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-[#1D3639]',
        pending: 'rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-slate-500',
      };

      const ICONS = {
        'Read / Accept Document': '<svg class="h-4 w-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-width="1.8" d="M6 3.75h8.25L18 7.5v12.75H6V3.75zM14 3.75V8h4M9 12h6M9 15h6"/></svg>',
        'Upload Document': '<svg class="h-4 w-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-width="1.8" d="M12 16V5m0 0L8 9m4-4l4 4M5 14v4.25A1.75 1.75 0 006.75 20h10.5A1.75 1.75 0 0019 18.25V14"/></svg>',
        'Keys / Access': '<svg class="h-4 w-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="8" cy="15" r="3"/><path stroke-width="1.8" d="M10.5 12.5L18 5m0 0v4m0-4h-4"/></svg>',
        Confirmation: '<svg class="h-4 w-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-width="1.8" d="M5 12l4 4L19 6"/></svg>',
        Action: '<svg class="h-4 w-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-width="1.8" d="M9 5l7 7-7 7"/></svg>',
      };

      const EDIT_ICON = '<svg class="h-4 w-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-width="1.8" d="M16.862 3.487a2.25 2.25 0 013.182 3.182L8.25 18.464 4 19.5l1.036-4.25L16.862 3.487z"/></svg>';
      const DELETE_ICON = '<svg class="h-4 w-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-width="1.8" d="M6 7h12M9 7V5h6v2M10 11v6M14 11v6M7 7l1 13h8l1-13"/></svg>';

      /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
       * Utilities
       * ---------------------------------------------------------------- */
      const byId = (id) => document.getElementById(id);

      const today = () =>
        new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-GB', { day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' });

      const icon = (type) => ICONS[type] || ICONS.Action;

      /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
       * Rendering — status view
       * ---------------------------------------------------------------- */
      function statusRow(list, item, index) {
        const toggleIcon = item.completed
          ? '<svg class="h-5 w-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-width="2" d="M5 12l4 4L19 7"/></svg>'
          : '<svg class="h-5 w-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="8"/></svg>';

        return `
          <div class="flex items-center gap-3 border-b border-slate-100 px-5 py-4 last:border-0 sm:px-6">
            <button
              onclick="toggleComplete('${list}', ${item.id})"
              title="${item.completed ? 'Mark outstanding' : 'Mark complete'}"
              class="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full ${
                item.completed ? 'bg-[#C3DF93]/50 text-[#1D3639]' : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-400'
              } hover:ring-2 hover:ring-[#1D3639]/10"
            >
              ${toggleIcon}
            </button>

            <div class="min-w-0 flex-1">
              <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                <p class="font-medium ${item.completed ? 'text-slate-900' : 'text-slate-600'}">
                  ${index + 1}. ${item.description}
                </p>
                <span class="rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-xs ${
                  item.required ? 'bg-[#FAF4DF] text-[#8a6f25]' : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-500'
                }">
                  ${item.required ? 'Required' : 'Optional'}
                </span>
              </div>
              <p class="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-400">
                ${item.type}${item.document ? ' · ' + item.document : ''}
              </p>
            </div>

            <div class="shrink-0 text-right">
              <p class="text-xs font-medium ${item.completed ? 'text-[#1D3639]' : 'text-[#E3572B]'}">
                ${item.completed ? 'Completed' : 'Outstanding'}
              </p>
              ${item.date ? `<p class="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-400">${item.date}</p>` : ''}
            </div>
          </div>`;
      }

      function renderStatus() {
        Object.entries(LISTS).forEach(([list, { prefix }]) => {
          const items = checklists[list];
          const completed = items.filter((i) => i.completed).length;
          const total = items.length;
          const percent = total ? Math.round((completed / total) * 100) : 0;
          const isComplete = completed === total;

          byId(`${prefix}Progress`).textContent = `${completed} of ${total}`;
          byId(`${prefix}Bar`).style.width = `${percent}%`;

          const status = byId(`${prefix}Status`);
          status.textContent = isComplete ? 'Complete' : 'In Progress';
          status.className = isComplete ? BADGE.complete : BADGE.pending;

          byId(`${prefix}List`).innerHTML = items.map((item, i) => statusRow(list, item, i)).join('');
        });
      }

      /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
       * Rendering — edit view
       * ---------------------------------------------------------------- */
      function editRow(list, item, index) {
        return `
          <div class="flex items-center gap-3 border-b border-slate-100 px-5 py-4 last:border-0 sm:px-6">
            <div class="flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500">
              ${index + 1}
            </div>

            <div class="min-w-0 flex-1">
              <p class="font-medium text-slate-800">${item.description}</p>
              <div class="mt-1 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                <span class="text-xs text-slate-400">${item.type}</span>
                ${item.document ? `<span class="text-xs text-[#1D3639]">· ${item.document}</span>` : ''}
                <span class="text-xs ${item.required ? 'text-[#8a6f25]' : 'text-slate-400'}">
                  ${item.required ? 'Required' : 'Optional'}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div class="flex gap-1">
              <button
                onclick="editItem('${list}', ${item.id})"
                class="rounded-lg p-2 text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-[#1D3639]"
              >
                ${EDIT_ICON}
              </button>
              <button
                onclick="deleteItem('${list}', ${item.id})"
                class="rounded-lg p-2 text-slate-400 hover:bg-red-50 hover:text-[#E3572B]"
              >
                ${DELETE_ICON}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>`;
      }

      function renderEdit() {
        Object.entries(LISTS).forEach(([list, { editTarget }]) => {
          byId(editTarget).innerHTML = checklists[list]
            .map((item, i) => editRow(list, item, i))
            .join('');
        });
      }

      /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
       * Rendering — tenant uploads
       * ---------------------------------------------------------------- */
      function uploadRow(file) {
        return `
          <div class="flex flex-col gap-3 border-b border-slate-100 px-5 py-4 last:border-0 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:px-6">
            <div class="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-slate-100 text-slate-500">
              <svg class="h-5 w-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                <path stroke-width="1.8" d="M6 3.75h8.25L18 7.5v12.75H6V3.75zM14 3.75V8h4M8.5 12h7M8.5 15h5"/>
              </svg>
            </div>

            <div class="min-w-0 flex-1">
              <p class="font-medium text-slate-800">${file.name}</p>
              <p class="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-400">${file.type} · ${file.size}</p>
            </div>

            <div class="flex items-center gap-4">
              <span class="text-xs text-slate-400">${file.date}</span>
              <button
                title="Open document"
                class="rounded-lg p-2 text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-[#1D3639]"
              >
                <svg class="h-4 w-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                  <path stroke-width="1.8" d="M14 5h5v5M19 5l-8 8M17 13v5H6V6h5"/>
                </svg>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>`;
      }

      function renderUploads() {
        byId('tenantUploads').innerHTML = uploads.length
          ? uploads.map(uploadRow).join('')
          : '<div class="text-md px-6 py-10 text-center text-slate-400">No documents have been uploaded by the tenant.</div>';
      }

      /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
       * Actions — status
       * ---------------------------------------------------------------- */
      function toggleComplete(list, id) {
        const item = checklists[list].find((i) => i.id === id);
        if (!item) return;

        item.completed = !item.completed;
        item.date = item.completed ? today() : null;
        renderStatus();
      }

      function toggleEdit() {
        editing = !editing;

        byId('statusView').classList.toggle('hidden', editing);
        byId('editView').classList.toggle('hidden', !editing);

        byId('editBtn').innerHTML = editing
          ? '<svg class="h-4 w-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-width="1.8" d="M5 12l4 4L19 7"/></svg><span>Done Editing</span>'
          : `${EDIT_ICON}<span>Edit Checklists</span>`;
      }

      /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
       * Actions — checklist items
       * ---------------------------------------------------------------- */
      function addItem(list) {
        byId('modalTitle').textContent = `Add ${LISTS[list].label} Item`;
        byId('itemId').value = '';
        byId('itemList').value = list;
        byId('itemDescription').value = '';
        byId('itemType').value = 'Action';
        byId('itemRequired').checked = true;
        byId('itemDocument').value = '';

        toggleDocumentField();
        openModal();
      }

      function editItem(list, id) {
        const item = checklists[list].find((i) => i.id === id);
        if (!item) return;

        byId('modalTitle').textContent = 'Edit Checklist Item';
        byId('itemId').value = id;
        byId('itemList').value = list;
        byId('itemDescription').value = item.description;
        byId('itemType').value = item.type;
        byId('itemRequired').checked = item.required;
        byId('itemDocument').value = item.document || '';

        toggleDocumentField();
        openModal();
      }

      function saveItem(event) {
        event.preventDefault();

        const id = byId('itemId').value;
        const list = byId('itemList').value;
        const type = byId('itemType').value;
        const description = byId('itemDescription').value.trim();
        const required = byId('itemRequired').checked;
        const document = type === 'Read / Accept Document' ? byId('itemDocument').value || null : null;

        if (id) {
          const item = checklists[list].find((i) => i.id === Number(id));
          if (item) Object.assign(item, { description, type, required, document });
        } else {
          checklists[list].push({
            id: Date.now(),
            description,
            type,
            document,
            required,
            completed: false,
            date: null,
          });
        }

        closeModal();
        render();
      }

      function deleteItem(list, id) {
        if (!confirm('Delete this checklist item?')) return;

        checklists[list] = checklists[list].filter((i) => i.id !== id);
        render();
      }

      function toggleDocumentField() {
        const isDocument = byId('itemType').value === 'Read / Accept Document';
        byId('documentField').classList.toggle('hidden', !isDocument);
      }

      /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
       * Modal
       * ---------------------------------------------------------------- */
      function openModal() {
        byId('itemModal').classList.remove('hidden');
        byId('itemModal').classList.add('flex');
      }

      function closeModal() {
        byId('itemModal').classList.add('hidden');
        byId('itemModal').classList.remove('flex');
      }

      byId('itemModal').addEventListener('click', (event) => {
        if (event.target.id === 'itemModal') closeModal();
      });

      /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
       * Init
       * ---------------------------------------------------------------- */
      function render() {
        renderStatus();
        renderEdit();
        renderUploads();
      }

      render();
    </script>
  </body>

<script src="../app.js"></script>
<script src="property.js"></script>

<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {

  const page = document.getElementById("page");
  if (page) requestAnimationFrame(() => page.classList.remove("opacity-0"));

  const openButtons = document.querySelectorAll("[data-modal-open]");
  const closeButtons = document.querySelectorAll("[data-modal-close]");

  function openModal(id) {
    const modal = document.getElementById(id);
    if (!modal) return;

    modal.classList.remove("hidden");
    modal.classList.add("flex");
    document.body.classList.add("overflow-hidden");
  }

  function closeModal(modal) {
    if (!modal) return;

    modal.classList.add("hidden");
    modal.classList.remove("flex");

    if (!document.querySelector(".fixed.inset-0.z-50.flex")) {
      document.body.classList.remove("overflow-hidden");
    }
  }

  openButtons.forEach(button => {
    button.addEventListener("click", () => openModal(button.dataset.modalOpen));
  });

  closeButtons.forEach(button => {
    button.addEventListener("click", () => closeModal(button.closest(".fixed")));
  });

  /* Close with Escape */
  document.addEventListener("keydown", event => {
    if (event.key !== "Escape") return;

    const modal = document.querySelector(".fixed.inset-0.z-50.flex");
    if (modal) closeModal(modal);
  });

  /* Prevent demo forms from refreshing the page */
  document.querySelectorAll(".fixed.inset-0.z-50 form").forEach(form => {
    form.addEventListener("submit", event => {
      event.preventDefault();
      closeModal(form.closest(".fixed.inset-0.z-50"));
    });
  });

});
</script>

<script>

document.querySelectorAll('[data-toggle]').forEach(toggle=>{

  toggle.addEventListener('click',()=>{

    const method=toggle.dataset.toggle;
    const enabled=toggle.getAttribute('aria-pressed')==='true';
    const next=!enabled;

    const details=document.getElementById(
      method==='bank'
        ? 'bankDetails'
        : method==='stripe'
          ? 'stripeDetails'
          : 'gocardlessDetails'
    );

    const knob=toggle.querySelector('span');

    toggle.setAttribute('aria-pressed',next);

    if(next){

      toggle.classList.remove('bg-slate-300');
      toggle.classList.add('bg-[#1D3639]');

      knob.classList.remove('left-1');
      knob.classList.add('left-6');

      details.classList.remove('hidden');

    }else{

      toggle.classList.remove('bg-[#1D3639]');
      toggle.classList.add('bg-slate-300');

      knob.classList.remove('left-6');
      knob.classList.add('left-1');

      details.classList.add('hidden');

    }

  });

});


window.addEventListener('load',()=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
  document.getElementById('page').classList.remove('opacity-0');
}));

</script>

fetch("property-header.html")
  .then(res => res.text())
  .then(html => {
    document.getElementById("propertyHeaderMount").innerHTML = html;

    const currentTab = document.body.dataset.activeTab || "home";
    
    function activateTab(tabKey) {
      document.querySelectorAll("[data-tab]").forEach(btn => {
        const isActive = btn.dataset.tab === tabKey;
        btn.classList.toggle("text-slate-900", isActive);
        btn.classList.toggle("border-slate-900", isActive);
        btn.classList.toggle("font-medium", isActive);
        btn.classList.toggle("text-slate-400", !isActive);
        btn.classList.toggle("border-transparent", !isActive);
      });
    }

    // Set initial page tab
    activateTab(currentTab);

  });<!-- /landlord/property.html -->

<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
  const page = document.getElementById("page");
  if (page) requestAnimationFrame(() => page.classList.remove("opacity-0", "scale-[0.96]"));

  const copyButton = document.getElementById("copyButton");
  const applicationUrl = "https://landlordship-auth.vercel.app/public/properties/enoch-homes";

  if (copyButton) {
    copyButton.addEventListener("click", async () => {
      try {
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(applicationUrl);

        const original = copyButton.innerHTML;
        copyButton.innerHTML = `
          <svg class="w-4 h-4 text-[#1D3639]" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
            <path stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="m5 12 4 4L19 6"/>
          </svg>
          Copied
        `;

        setTimeout(() => {
          copyButton.innerHTML = original;
        }, 1500);

      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Could not copy application link:", error);
      }
    });
  }
});
</script>

  <script>
    function showTab(tab,button){
      document.querySelectorAll('.tab-content').forEach(el=>el.classList.add('hidden'));
      document.getElementById('tab-'+tab).classList.remove('hidden');
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-tab]').forEach(el=>{
        el.classList.remove('text-slate-900','font-medium','border-slate-900');
        el.classList.add('text-slate-400','border-transparent');
      });
      button.classList.remove('text-slate-400','border-transparent');
      button.classList.add('text-slate-900','font-medium','border-slate-900');
    }

    function openModal(id){
      const modal=document.getElementById(id);
      modal.classList.remove('hidden');
      modal.classList.add('flex');
    }

    function closeModal(id){
      const modal=document.getElementById(id);
      modal.classList.add('hidden');
      modal.classList.remove('flex');
    }

    function toggleSwitch(el){
      const dot=el.querySelector('span');
      const active=el.classList.contains('bg-slate-900');
      el.classList.toggle('bg-slate-900',!active);
      el.classList.toggle('bg-slate-200',active);
      dot.classList.toggle('left-6',!active);
      dot.classList.toggle('left-1',active);
    }

    document.querySelectorAll('[id$="Modal"]').forEach(modal=>{
      modal.addEventListener('click',e=>{
        if(e.target===modal) closeModal(modal.id);
      });
    });

    document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
      if(e.key==='Escape'){
        document.querySelectorAll('[id$="Modal"]').forEach(modal=>closeModal(modal.id));
      }
    });
  </script>

  <script src="app.js"></script>

<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded",()=>{const page=document.getElementById("page");if(page)requestAnimationFrame(()=>page.classList.remove("opacity-0"));const email=document.getElementById("emailInput"),search=document.getElementById("searchBtn"),result=document.getElementById("tenantResult"),notFound=document.getElementById("notFound"),error=document.getElementById("searchError"),continueBtn=document.getElementById("continueBtn"),searchStep=document.getElementById("searchStep"),propertyStep=document.getElementById("propertyStep"),backBtn=document.getElementById("backBtn"),assignBtn=document.getElementById("assignBtn"),property=document.getElementById("propertySelect"),propertyError=document.getElementById("propertyError");const showTenant=()=>{const value=email.value.trim().toLowerCase();result.classList.add("hidden");notFound.classList.add("hidden");error.classList.add("hidden");if(!value){error.textContent="Please enter a tenant email address.";error.classList.remove("hidden");return}if(!value.includes("@")){error.textContent="Please enter a valid email address.";error.classList.remove("hidden");return}if(value==="daniel.williams@email.com"){document.getElementById("tenantName").textContent="Daniel Williams";document.getElementById("tenantEmail").textContent=value;result.classList.remove("hidden")}else{notFound.classList.remove("hidden")}};search.addEventListener("click",showTenant);email.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{if(e.key==="Enter")showTenant()});continueBtn.addEventListener("click",()=>{document.getElementById("selectedTenantName").textContent=document.getElementById("tenantName").textContent;document.getElementById("selectedTenantEmail").textContent=document.getElementById("tenantEmail").textContent;searchStep.classList.add("hidden");propertyStep.classList.remove("hidden");document.getElementById("step1Indicator").classList.add("text-slate-400");document.getElementById("step1Indicator").classList.remove("text-[#1D3639]");document.getElementById("step2Indicator").classList.remove("text-slate-400");document.getElementById("step2Indicator").classList.add("text-[#1D3639]");document.querySelector("#step2Indicator span").classList.remove("bg-slate-100");document.querySelector("#step2Indicator span").classList.add("bg-[#1D3639]","text-white")});backBtn.addEventListener("click",()=>{propertyStep.classList.add("hidden");searchStep.classList.remove("hidden")});assignBtn.addEventListener("click",()=>{if(!property.value){propertyError.classList.remove("hidden");return}assignBtn.textContent="Assigning...";setTimeout(()=>window.location.href="../properties/property-tenancy.html",700)});property.addEventListener("change",()=>propertyError.classList.add("hidden"))});
</script>
